```
你在 ~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game（Terraria 1.4.5.6 网页复刻）。任务：**只读调查**（禁止编辑任何文件）用户报告的地牢入口异常，输出诊断报告。

## 用户报告（世界名"优雅地盘"，debug 报告 ~/Downloads/debug-report-优雅地盘-2026-08-12T08-19-20-193Z.json）
1. 地牢入口（地表走廊，地牢守卫老人所在处）经常**被水淹**；
2. 或被**泥土/沙覆盖**；
3. 地牢**塔楼顶偶发被削平**。

## 背景与首要嫌疑（先验证这个）
种子等价工程已证实执行序=注册序，我们链有一个已备案的管线倒挂：原版序 Lakes(#30,cs:14613) 在 Dungeon(#32,cs:14816) **之前**；我们的 Dungeon 在 vanillaBiomes 段内、Lakes 在其后的"液体"槽 → **湖泊在地牢建成后才挖**，理论上正好能淹入口。另一个代理此刻正在转录/修这段（#27-32），你**不要编辑任何 gen 文件**，只诊断。

## 调查步骤
1. 解析 debug 报告 JSON（参考另一会话的做法：版本 3 idScheme sheet，w/h 4200×1200，tile RLE。可写临时脚本到 /tmp 执行，勿写仓库目录）。找到地牢入口位置（dungeonX 可从报告或用砖族 41/43/44 扫描定位），检查入口走廊的液体格、覆盖格类型分布、塔楼顶行完整性。
2. 对照 Terarria1456 反编译源码（~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/）：
   - Lakes pass（cs:14613）挖湖条件——是否会命中地牢入口区域（vanilla 里 dungeon 在 lakes 之后所以无此问题）；
   - Dungeon pass（cs:14816/MakeDungeon）入口走廊建造后，原版有没有防后续 pass 破坏的门禁（如 CanEvilReplace 类似物、dungeon 区域保护）；
   - 原版 SmoothWorld（cs:16507）/Waterfalls 等会不会削塔顶——塔顶被削是原版语义还是我们的偏差；
   - 泥土/沙覆盖候选：我们链里 Dungeon 之后的 pass（beaches 沙化、grass、settle liquids 前的 water 移动等）。
3. 用 vitest/临时脚本生成同种子世界验证（`npx vitest run` 现有测试可跑；写一次性脚本放 /tmp，用 node --experimental-strip-types 或 vitest 环境跑都行，**不得新增仓库内测试文件或修改源码**）。确认：入口被淹是否稳定复现、被淹的水是 Lakes 的湖还是 beaches 的海/瀑布水。
4. 给出结论：每类异常（淹/埋/削顶）的根因 pass + 是"我们的管线序偏差"还是"JS 实现缺陷"还是"原版语义如此"，附源码行号依据。

## 产出
一份诊断报告（最终消息直接输出）：① 报告 JSON 里地牢入口/塔顶的实测 tile 状态；② 三类异常各自根因+行号；③ 修复建议（写清楚该谁修：正在跑的 #27-32 转录代理会修倒挂，还是需要单独修）；④ 与对账工程的关联（倒挂修正后这些异常是否自然消失的预判）。
```

```
5://   - 区域转储 = 行 RLE 串：cell = "sheet:fx:fy:wall:liquid"，空格记 "0"，连续相同 cell 记 "cell*runLen"，行内以 ";" 分隔
44:export interface RleAreaDump { x0: number; y0: number; w: number; h: number; rows: string[] }
58:// ================= RLE 编解码（导出供测试往返） =================
60:/** 一行 cell token 序列 → RLE 串（"0"、"sheet:fx:fy:wall:liquid"，连续相同合并 *runLen） */
67:    if (runTok) parts.push(runLen > 1 ? `${runTok}*${runLen}` : runTok);
71:  if (runTok) parts.push(runLen > 1 ? `${runTok}*${runLen}` : runTok);
75:/** RLE 串 → cell token 序列（与 encodeRle 互逆） */
83:    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) out.push(tok);
116:/** 区域转储：矩形逐行 RLE（出界裁剪到世界内） */
125:  const rows: string[] = [];
128:    for (let x = bx0; x <= bx1; x++) toks.push(cellToken(st, st.idx(x, y)));
129:    rows.push(encodeRle(toks));
131:  return { x0: bx0, y0: by0, w: bx1 - bx0 + 1, h: by1 - by0 + 1, rows };
255:  grid: [number, number]; stride: [number, number]; cols: number; rows: number;
282:    cols: number; rows: number;
409:      } else if (m.cols > 0 && m.rows > 0) {
410:        // 表未载：按元数据网格判定（cols/rows × stride 的像素范围）
411:        oob = sx < 0 || sy < 0 || sx + sw > m.cols * m.stride[0] || sy + sh > m.rows * m.stride[1];
418:        cols: m.cols, rows: m.rows,
429:    const hPx = meta ? meta.rows * meta.stride[1] : 0;
529:      if (s) inv.push([i, s.id, s.stack, s.prefix ?? 0, ITEM_DEFS[s.id]?.vid ?? -1]);
533:      if (s) equip.push([i, s.id, ITEM_DEFS[s.id]?.vid ?? -1]);
```

```json
{
 "command": "cd /tmp && cat > decode_area.py <<'EOF'\nimport json, sys\nd = json.load(open('~/Downloads/debug-report-优雅地盘-2026-08-12T08-19-20-193Z.json'))\ndef decode(rows, x0, y0):\n    grid = []\n    for ri, row in enumerate(rows):\n        cells = []\n        for part in row.split(';'):\n            if '*' in part:\n                tok, n = part.split('*'); n = int(n)\n            else:\n                tok, n = part, 1\n            f = tok.split(':')\n            if len(f) == 5:\n                t, fx, fy, wall, liq = int(f[0]), int(f[1]), int(f[2]), int(f[3]), int(f[4])\n            else:\n                t, fx, fy, wall, liq = -1, 0, 0, 0, 0\n            for _ in range(n):\n                cells.append((t, fx, fy, wall, liq))\n        grid.append(cells)\n    return grid\n\nNAMES = {-1:'?',0:'.',1:'S',2:'G',3:'P',4:'T',5:'s',53:'a',39:'?',40:'?',41:'B',43:'b',44:'D',59:'m',60:'g',75:'?',112:'?'}\ndef name(t):\n    return NAMES.get(t, str(t) if t<10 else chr(65+(t%26))+str(t//26) if t>=100 else NAMES.get(t,'?'))\na = d['tiles']['areaCamera']\ng = decode(a['rows'], a['x0'], a['y0'])\nprint(f\"camera area x0={a['x0']} y0={a['y0']} w={a['w']} h={a['h']} len0={len(g[0])}\")\n# print header col every 10\nhdr = '    ' + ''.join(str((a['x0']+i)%10) if (a['x0']+i)%10==0 else ' ' for i in range(len(g[0])))\nprint(hdr)\nfor ri, row in enumerate(g):\n    y = a['y0']+ri\n    line = ''.join(name(c[0]) for c in row)\n    # mark liquid\n    liqline = ''.join('~' if c[4]>0 else ' ' for c in row)\n    print(f\"{y:4d} {line}  {liqline.strip()}\")\nEOF\npython3 decode_area.py | head -60",
 "description": "Decode camera area RLE into ASCII grid"
}
```

```json
{
 "command": "cd /tmp && cat > decode2.py <<'EOF'\nimport json\nd = json.load(open('~/Downloads/debug-report-优雅地盘-2026-08-12T08-19-20-193Z.json'))\ndef decode(rows):\n    grid = []\n    for row in rows:\n        cells = []\n        for part in row.split(';'):\n            if '*' in part:\n                tok, n = part.split('*'); n = int(n)\n            else: tok, n = part, 1\n            f = tok.split(':')\n            if len(f) == 5:\n                cells.extend([(int(f[0]), int(f[1]), int(f[2]), int(f[3]), int(f[4]))]*n)\n            else:\n                cells.extend([(-1,0,0,0,0)]*n)\n        grid.append(cells)\n    return grid\na = d['tiles']['areaCamera']; g = decode(a['rows'])\nx0,y0,w,h = a['x0'],a['y0'],a['w'],a['h']\n# legend: air=' ', 0 dirt '.', 1 stone 'S', 2 grass 'G', 41 'B', 43 'b', 44 'd', 53 sand 'a'\nLEG={-1:' ',0:'.',1:'S',2:'G',53:'a',59:'m',60:'j',23:'I',25:'C',41:'B',43:'b',44:'d'}\ndef ch(t):\n    return LEG.get(t, 'x' if t>=100 else ('0123456789')[t%10] if t<10 else chr(97+t%26))\nprint('header x:', x0, '-', x0+w-1, ' y:', y0, '-', y0+h-1)\nhdr = '     ' + ''.join(str((x0+i)//100%10) for i in range(w))\nprint(hdr)\nhdr2 = '     ' + ''.join(str((x0+i)//10%10) for i in range(w))\nprint(hdr2)\nhdr3 = '     ' + ''.join(str((x0+i)%10) for i in range(w))\nprint(hdr3)\nfrom collections import Counter\ncnt = Counter()\nfor ri,row in enumerate(g):\n    y=y0+ri\n    tline=''.join(ch(c[0]) for c in row)\n    lline=''.join('~' if c[4]>0 else (' ' if c[0]!=-1 else ' ') for c in row)\n    wline=''.join('#' if c[3]>0 else ' ' for c in row)\n    for c in row: cnt[c[0]]+=1\n    print(f\"{y:4d} {tline} |{lline}|{wline}\")\nprint('tile histogram:', sorted(cnt.items(), key=lambda kv:-kv[1]))\nEOF\npython3 decode2.py",
 "description": "Decode camera area with liquids and walls overlay"
}
```

```
function vanillaLakes({ store, rng }: GenCtx) {
  // Lakes(30)/Slush(31) 已按注册序并入 vanillaBiomes（Corruption(29) 后、Dungeon(32) 前）
  // ——本槽保留空壳防外部引用。
  void store; void rng;
  const gs = ctxGs[0];
  if (!gs) return;
}

function vanillaCaves({ store, rng }: GenCtx) {
  const gs = ctxGs[0];
  if (!gs) return;
  // 原版注册序（1456）：OceanSand(11603) → SandPatches(11712) → Tunnels(11743) →
  // MountainCaves(11809) → DirtWallBackgrounds(11883) → RocksInDirt/DirtInRocks/Clay
  // → SmallHoles(12034) → DirtLayer/RockLayer/Surface Caves（WavyCaves 12301 仅
  // 饥荒世界，普通世界零掷骰跳过）
  runOceanSandPass(store, rng, gs);
  runSandPatchesPass(store, rng, gs);   // 地下沙团（沙漠雏形）
  runTunnelsPass(store, rng, gs);       // 地表隧道（dirt runner 双向填充）
  runMountCavesPass(store, rng, gs);    // 山丘本体
  runDirtWallBgPass(store, rng, gs);    // 地表自然墙（wall 2/40）
  runRocksAndClayPass(store, rng, gs);
  runSmallHolesPass(store, rng, gs);    // 小空洞（每轮 2 发挖除 runner）
  runCavesPass(store, rng, gs);
}

// 原版 1456 真实注册序（执行序=注册序，WorldGenerator.cs:447 _passes[PassResults.Count]）：
//   14 SurfaceCaves → 15 WavyCaves(仅饥荒世界,普通零消耗) → 16 IceBiome → 17 Grass
//   → 18 MudCaves → 19 Desert → 20 Mushroom → 21 Marble → 22 Granite → 23 FloatingIslands
//   → 24 DirtToMud → 25 Silt → 26 OresAndShinies → 27 Webs(未移植) → 28 Underworld
//   → 29 Corruption → 30 Lakes → 31 Slush → 32 Dungeon → 33 MountainCaveOpenings
//   → 34 Beaches → 35 Gems → 36 GravitatingSand → 37 OceanCaves → 38 Shimmer → 39 DirtWallCleanup
// 本函数内对齐到 16-35 段（Underworld 28 在管线末"地狱"槽、Webs 27 后置——既有偏差另案）。
function vanillaBiomes({ store, rng, world, surface }: GenCtx) {
  const gs = ctxGs[0];
  if (!gs) return;
  runIceBiomePass(store, rng, gs);       // 16 Generate Ice Biome
  runGrassPass(store, rng, gs);          // 17 Grass
  const jx = runJunglePass(store, rng, gs); // JunglePass
  world.jungleX = jx;
  // 丛林表面泥化:泥柱核心区 ±0.12w 内,表层 10 格 dirt→mud(原版巨型 runner
  // 覆盖表面所致;我们补这一步让丛林表面成草皮而非裸土)
  {
    const core = (gs as any).jungleCoreX ?? jx;
    const reach = Math.floor(store.w * 0.12);
    for (let x = Math.max(0, core - reach); x < Math.min(store.w, core + reach); x++) {
      let sy = 0;
      while (sy < store.h - 12 && !store.flags[store.idx(x, sy)]) sy++;
      for (let y = sy; y < sy + 10; y++) {
        if (store.get(x, y) === T.DIRT) store.setTileSilent(x, y, TILE_BY_KEY['mud']!);
      }
    }
  }
  // 18 Mud Caves To Grass（cs:12499-12530 原版精确版：全图递归洪水+散块清除；
  // 曾用近似 spreadGrassAll 中心限距+2 轮快照——caves-oracle 位级对账后替换）
  runMudCavesPass(store, TILE_BY_KEY['mud']!, TILE_BY_KEY['v_60_jungle_grass_block']!);
  runDesertPass(store, rng, gs);         // 19 Full Desert
  runMushroomPass(store, rng, gs);       // 20 Mushroom Patches
  runMarbleGranitePass(store, rng, gs);  // 21/22 Marble/Granite
  runFloatingIslandsPass(store, rng, gs); // 23 Floating Islands（原在蘑菇前——注册序修正）
  runDirtToMudAndSiltPass(store, rng, gs); // 24 Dirt To Mud / 25 Silt
  runShiniesPass(store, rng, gs);        // 26 Ores And Shinies（原在很后"矿石"槽——移到 Silt 后=Webs 位）
  runVanillaWebsPass(store, rng, gs);     // 27 Webs（cs:13659——原后置防被清，注册序修正回 27 位）
  runUnderworldPass(store, rng, gs);     // 28 Underworld（cs:13697——原管线末"地狱"槽；
                                        //  地狱屋/地狱箱/熔炉等依赖槽在数组其后 = 地狱 pass 之后 ✓）
  runCorruptionPass(store, rng, gs);     // 29 Corruption/Crimson
  runLakesPass(store, rng, gs);          // 30 Lakes（cs:14613——原"液体"槽在结构段后，注册序修正）
  runSlushPass(store, gs);               // 31 StoneToIceAndSiltPlusMudIntoSlush（cs:14756，紧随 Lakes）
  runDungeonPass(store, rng, gs, world.chests); // 32 Dungeon(蓝/粉砖主题+走廊房间+宝箱)
  // 地牢 pass 预计算会重掷 dungeonLocation(锚点 ±300 拒绝采样后迁移)——必须在此后
  // 回写最终位置,否则 world.dungeonX 停留在 Reset 期的旧值(与实际地牢偏差可达数百格)。
  // 优先取入口门厅可站立点(原版 dungeonX/Y 语义 cs L72510-72527:入口结构上可站处,
  // 守卫老人出生/游走锚)——塔心锚点会把老人漏进塔内
  world.dungeonX = gs.dungeonEntX || gs.dungeonLocation;
  world.dungeonY = gs.dungeonEntY || gs.dungeonY || 0;
  // 33 MountainCaveOpenings + 34 Beaches（原紧跟 SurfaceCaves——注册序修正移到 Dungeon 后）
  runMountainCavesPass(store, rng, gs);
  runBeachesPass(store, rng, gs);
  // 海盆挖完后刷新 surface[](后续旧 pass 依赖;本函数之后才到那些 pass)
  for (let x = 0; x < store.w; x++) {
    for (let y = 0; y < store.h; y++) {
      if (store.flags[store.idx(x, y)]) { surface[x] = y; break; }
    }
  }
  runGemsPass(store, rng, gs);           // 35 Gems（原捆绑在 Lakes 槽——注册序修正移到 Beaches 后）
  // 原版 1456 注册序（WorldGen.cs 行号 = 唯一权威，2026-08-11 review 依此重排）：
  //   OceanCaves(15228) < Pyramids(15426) < SurfaceWaterInJungle(15887) <
  //   Temple(15911) < Beehives(16005) < JungleShrines(16059) < Statues(16947) <
  //   UndergroundHouses+BuriedChests(17066) < SurfaceChests(17226) <
  //   ChestsInJungleShrines(17308) < UnderwaterChests(17343) < SpiderCaves(17455) <
  //   FloatingIslandHouses(17971) < MicroBiomes(21785)
  // 神庙/灌水提前到宝箱系之前（原版语义:大结构先占位,箱/雕像后放才不会塞进
  // 神庙体内或被灌水淹没;此前神庙在蜘蛛洞后 → 占地区内已塞雕像/箱,放置互相破坏）。
  const oceanTreasures = runOceanCavesPass(store, rng, gs); // 海洋洞窟蠕虫+宝藏点记录
  runPyramidPass(store, rng, gs, world.chests); // Pyramids(沙岩砖金字塔+宝箱)
  // 原版注册序 Pyramids(15426) < LivingTrees(15551) < LivingTreeWalls(15792) <
  // SurfaceWaterInJungle(15887)——世界树插在金字塔后、丛林灌水前（方案 A 原版位置）。
  // LivingTrees：GrowLivingTree 树干壳+枝叶+根系+贯通竖井+密室宝箱；LivingTreeWalls：
  // 全图 type==191 连通核补墙 244（零掷骰）。
  runLivingTreesPass(store, rng, gs, world.chests);  // Living Trees(世界树)
  runLivingTreeWallsPass(store, gs);                 // Living Tree Walls(活木墙)
  runWetJunglePass(store, rng, gs);             // SurfaceWaterInJungle(1456 cs:15887 逐列灌水,零 RNG)
  runTemplePass(store, rng, gs, world.chests);  // Jungle Temple(之字形房间链+走廊+门+宝箱)
  runBeehivePass(store, rng, gs);               // Hives(丛林蜂巢:蜂巢壳+蜂蜜+腔)
  const shrineSpots = runJungleShrinePass(store, rng, gs);  // 丛林神龛(壳+腔+火把+金字塔顶)
  runStatuesPass(store, rng, gs);                           // 雕像 73 序+陷阱雕像接线
  runBuriedChestsPass(store, rng, gs, world.chests); // Buried Chests(洞穴金/冰箱/沙箱;地狱箱已拆到地狱箱 pass)
  runCaveHousesPass(store, rng, gs, world.chests);   // 地下小屋七主题(木/冰/丛林/沙/花岗岩/大理石/蘑菇)
  runSurfaceChestsPass(store, rng, gs, world.chests); // Surface Chests(地表木箱;只放一次——曾双调致密度翻倍)
  runJungleChestsPass(store, rng, gs, world.chests, shrineSpots); // 常春藤箱(神龛中心)
  runUnderwaterChestsPass(store, rng, gs, world.chests, oceanTreasures); // 海洋洞窟水箱
  runWaterChestsPass(store, rng, gs, world.chests); // Water Chests(水中 style17;1.4.4 概念保留)
  // Webs(51) 已按注册序回到 #27 位（vanillaBiomes 前段）——此处旧迟置调用删除
  runSpiderNestPass(store, rng, gs, world.chests); // Spider Caves(countTiles 门+波前BFS刷墙62+蛛网罐/箱/垂网/覆饰)
  runWebsInSpiderCavesPass(store, rng, gs);     // Webs In Spider Caves(墙62 9/10蛛网51)+蜂巢钟乳石/蜂蜜
  runIslandHousePass(store, rng, gs, world.chests); // 浮空岛日光板房屋(Skyware 门+箱+桌椅)
  runSwordShrinePass(store, rng, gs);          // 附魔剑圣地(竖井+水潭洞+土丘剑)
```

```
14	const EBONSTONE = TILE_BY_KEY['v_25_ebonstone_block'] ?? -1;  // 原版 25=黑檀石（曾误当 chest）
15	
16	/** WillWaterPlacedHereStayPut 近似：下方实心且自身无液体 → 水放得住 */
17	function willWaterStayPut(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {
18	  return !!st.inBounds(x, y + 1) && !!st.flags[st.idx(x, y + 1)] && st.liquid[st.idx(x, y)] === 0;
19	}
20	
21	/** SonOfLakinater（L48516）：向下蜿蜒湖腔。lava 恒 false（getGoodWorld 不支持） */
22	function sonOfLakinater(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, i: number, j: number, strengthMultiplier = 1): void {
23	  // cs:80357 头部 Next(3)（liquidType——普通世界恒水，但掷骰必消耗！曾缺失致流偏移）
24	  rng.int(0, 2);
25	  let radius = rng.int(15, 30) * strengthMultiplier;   // num1（1/5 ×1.3）
26	  let steps = rng.int(30, 60);                         // num2
27	  if (rng.int(0, 4) === 0) { radius *= 1.3; steps *= 1.3; }
28	  let px = i + 0.0, py = j + 0.0;
29	  let wander = rng.next() * (1 / 500);                 // num3
30	  let vx: number;
31	  if (rng.int(0, 3) !== 0) {
32	    vx = rng.int(-15, 15) * 0.01;
33	  } else {
34	    vx = rng.int(-50, 50) * 0.01;
35	    wander = rng.next() * 0.004 + 0.001;
36	  }
37	  let vy = rng.int(0, 100) * 0.01;
38	  const stepsTotal = steps;                            // num4
39	  while (radius > 3 && steps > 0) {
40	    radius -= rng.int(0, 10) * 0.1;
41	    steps--;
42	    let x0 = Math.floor(px - radius * 4), x1 = Math.floor(px + radius * 4);
43	    let y0 = Math.floor(py - radius * 3), y1 = Math.floor(py + radius * 2);
44	    if (x0 < 0) x0 = 0;
45	    if (x1 > st.w) x1 = st.w;
46	    if (y0 < 0) y0 = 0;
47	    if (y1 > st.h) y1 = st.h;
48	    const r = radius;                                  // num9
49	    const entranceY = j + 5;                           // num17
50	    for (let x = x0; x < x1; x++) {
51	      for (let y = y0; y < y1; y++) {
52	        if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
53	        const ti = st.idx(x, y);
54	        const t = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, steps / stepsTotal)); // num2/num4（lerp 系数）
55	        const xc = (Math.abs(x - px) * 0.6) * (1 - t) + (Math.abs(x - px) * 0.3) * t;
56	        const yc = (Math.abs(y - py) * 1.4) * (1 - t) + (Math.abs(y - py) * 5) * t;
57	        const dist = Math.hypot(xc, yc);               // num16
58	        if (dist < r * 0.4) {
59	          // 湖腔主体：掏空 + 灌水
60	          if (y >= j) {
61	            const ti2 = st.idx(x, Math.min(st.h - 1, y));
62	            if (y <= j + 1 ? willWaterStayPut(st, x, y) : true) {
63	              st.liquid[ti2] = 255;
64	              st.liquidType[ti2] = 1;
65	            }
66	          }
67	          const wasGrass = st.flags[ti] && (st.type[ti] === MUD || st.type[ti] === JUNGLE_GRASS);
68	          st.setTileSilent(x, y, 0);
69	          // 原版此处 SpreadGrass 邻格（59/60 转草）——草传播未移植，跳过（后续 pass 铺草）
70	          void wasGrass;
71	        } else if (y > j + 1 && dist < r && st.liquid[ti] === 0) {
72	          // 腔壁泥土壳：窄处且有墙三面包围 → 填实
73	          if (Math.abs(x - px) * 0.8 < r && st.wall[ti] > 0
74	            && st.wall[st.idx(x - 1, y)] > 0 && st.wall[st.idx(x + 1, y)] > 0
75	            && st.wall[st.idx(x, y + 1)] > 0) {
76	            st.flags[ti] = 1;
77	          }
78	        } else if (y < j && steps === stepsTotal - 1 && y > gs.worldSurfaceLow - 20) {
79	          // 首步：向上漏斗入口（L48617-48638）
80	          const cloud = TILE_BY_KEY['v_189_cloud_block']!;
81	          if (!(st.flags[ti] && (st.type[ti] === cloud || st.type[ti] === TILE_BY_KEY['v_196_rain_cloud_block']))) {
82	            const fx = Math.abs(x - i) * 0.7;
83	            const fy = Math.abs(y - entranceY) * 1.35;
84	            const width = (1 - Math.abs(x - i) / Math.max(1, x1 - i)) * 2.3;
85	            const pow = width ** 4;
86	            if (y < entranceY && fx < r * 0.4 + Math.abs(y - entranceY) * 0.5 * pow) {
87	              st.setTileSilent(x, y, 0);
88	            }
89	          }
90	        }
91	      }
92	    }
93	    px += vx; py += vy;
94	    vx += rng.int(-100, 100) * wander;
95	    vy += rng.int(-100, 100) * 0.01;
96	    if (vx > 1) vx = 1;
97	    if (vx < -1) vx = -1;
98	    if (vy > 1) vy = 1;
99	    const floor = 0.5 * (1 - steps / stepsTotal);
100	    if (vy < floor) vy = floor;
101	  }
102	}

104	/** pass 31 Lakes（L6450-6560）：num3 = Next(n*3, n*6) 次尝试（n=w/4200），上限 maxLakes-1 */
105	export function runLakesPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
106	  const { w } = st;
107	  const n = Math.floor(w / 4200);
108	  const tries = rng.int(n * 3, n * 6 - 1);             // Next(n*3, n*6)
109	  const beachAvoid = 275 + 5 + 40 + 20;                // lakesBeachAvoidance = beachSandRandomCenter(320)+20
110	  const maxLakes = 50;
111	  const lakeX: number[] = [];
112	  let budget = Math.floor(w / 4);                      // num4 尝试预算
113	  for (let k = 0; k < tries; k++) {
114	    if (lakeX.length >= maxLakes - 1) break;
115	    while (budget > 0) {
116	      let bad = false;
117	      budget--;
118	      let x = rng.int(beachAvoid, w - beachAvoid - 1); // Next(avoid, w-avoid)
119	      while (x > w * 0.45 && x < w * 0.55) {
120	        x = rng.int(beachAvoid, w - beachAvoid - 1);
121	      }
122	      for (const lx of lakeX) { if (Math.abs(x - lx) < 150) { bad = true; break; } }
123	      if (!bad) {
124	        for (let c = 0; c < gs.numMCaves; c++) {
125	          if (Math.abs(x - gs.mCaveX[c]) < 100) { bad = true; break; }
126	        }
127	      }
128	      // cs:14660-14666：隧道 ±100 避让（GenVars.tunnelX/numTunnels——曾缺失致首湖错位）
129	      if (!bad) {
130	        for (let c = 0; c < gs.numTunnels; c++) {
131	          if (Math.abs(x - gs.tunnelX[c]) < 100) { bad = true; break; }
132	        }
133	      }
134	      if (bad) continue;
135	      // 自 worldSurfaceLow-20 向下找实心
136	      let y = gs.worldSurfaceLow - 20;
137	      for (;;) {
138	        if (st.flags[st.idx(x, y)]) break;
139	        y++;
140	        if (y >= gs.worldSurface || st.wall[st.idx(x, y)] > 0) { bad = true; break; }
141	      }
142	      if (!bad && st.type[st.idx(x, y)] === SAND) bad = true;
143	      if (bad) continue;
144	      // ±50 无猩红石/宝箱
145	      for (let dx = -50; dx <= 50 && !bad; dx++) {
146	        for (let dy = -50; dy <= 50; dy++) {
147	          if (!st.inBounds(x + dx, y + dy)) continue;
148	          const t = st.type[st.idx(x + dx, y + dy)];
149	          // 原版 cs:14690 不判 active：type==203/25（猩红石/黑檀石）即拒
150	          if (t === CRIMSTONE || t === EBONSTONE) { bad = true; break; }
151	        }
152	      }
153	      if (bad) continue;
154	      // 两侧 ±20 皆实心为止下沉（上限 worldSurface-50）
155	      const anchor = y;
156	      const solidAt = (xx: number, yy: number) => st.inBounds(xx, yy) && st.flags[st.idx(xx, yy)];
157	      while (!solidAt(x - 20, y) || !solidAt(x + 20, y)) {
158	        y++;
159	        if (y > gs.worldSurface - 50) { bad = true; break; }
160	      }
161	      if (bad || y - anchor <= 10) continue;
162	      // ±60 顶部 20 格净空（无块无墙）
163	      for (let dx = -60; dx <= 60 && !bad; dx++) {
164	        const ty = y - 20;
165	        if (!st.inBounds(x + dx, ty)) continue;
166	        if (st.flags[st.idx(x + dx, ty)] || st.wall[st.idx(x + dx, ty)] > 0) bad = true;
167	      }
168	      if (bad) continue;
169	      // 121×121 实心率 ≥80%
170	      let solidN = 0;
171	      for (let dx = -60; dx <= 60; dx++) {
172	        for (let dy = 0; dy <= 120; dy++) {
173	          if (solidAt(x + dx, y + dy)) solidN++;
174	        }
175	      }
176	      if (solidN < 121 * 121 * 0.8) continue;
177	      // 非地下沙漠 ±8（无记录区间，墙 187/216/217 近似）
178	      let inDesert = false;
179	      for (let dx = -8; dx <= 8 && !inDesert; dx++) {
180	        for (let dy = -8; dy <= 8; dy++) {
181	          if (!st.inBounds(x + dx, y + dy)) continue;
182	          const wl = st.wall[st.idx(x + dx, y + dy)];
183	          if (wl === 187 || wl === 216 || wl === 217) { inDesert = true; break; }
184	        }
185	      }
186	      if (inDesert) continue;
187	      sonOfLakinater(st, rng, gs, x, y, 1);
188	      lakeX.push(x);
189	      break;
190	    }
191	  }
192	  gs.lakeX = lakeX;
193	}
194
```

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-12T08:24:10.358Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
Our LakesPass digs a surface lake starting at y = worldSurfaceLow-20 going down, filling with water (liquid=255). The lake "funnel entrance" is dug upward to the surface (`y < j && steps === stepsTotal - 1` branch digs tiles up to `gs.worldSurfaceLow - 20`... it digs at y > worldSurfaceLow-20 only, so just below the surface low point — that opens a hole to the surface). Then the lake cavity extends downward from y=j.

If a lake lands near the dungeon entrance, water at liquid 255 fills the cavity and the surface hole. If the dungeon was already built (old inverted order), the lake dig would carve through the entrance corridor and fill it with water → flooding. If the dungeon is built AFTER (current fixed order), the dungeon pass would overwrite — but does OUR DungeonPass clear liquid? That's the crucial question.

Also note: our Lakes dig doesn't avoid the dungeon at all — vanilla doesn't either, relying on order. With the current fixed order (Lakes #30 before Dungeon #32), vanilla semantics apply: dungeon digs over it.

But wait — there's a subtlety: the water. In vanilla, when the dungeon is built after the lake, the dungeon carves air where there was water, and ClearEverything/liquid=0 removes it. But if our DungeonPass's carving doesn't reset liquid, water would remain in the corridors → entrance flooded even with correct order. Let me check DungeonPass.ts for liquid handling.
```

</details>

```
// 地牢生成验证：完整管线生成后，地牢内部应存在 7/8/9 墙 + 地牢砖；
// world.dungeonX/Y 应指向实际结构（DungeonPass 重掷后的最终位置）
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { generateWorld } from '../src/world/gen/WorldGen';

describe('地牢生成（DungeonPass）', () => {
  it('完整管线后地牢墙存活 + dungeonX/Y 指向结构', async () => {
    const world = await generateWorld({ width: 4200, height: 1200, seedText: 'dungeon-probe-1' });
    const st = world.store;
    const dX = world.dungeonX, dY = world.dungeonY || Math.floor(world.groundLevel);
    // 扫地牢锚点周边 ±120 × (dY-20 .. rockLevel+200)
    let dungeonWalls = 0, dungeonBricks = 0;
    const y1 = Math.min(st.h - 10, Math.floor(world.rockLevel) + 200);
    for (let y = Math.max(10, dY - 20); y < y1; y++) {
      for (let x = Math.max(2, dX - 120); x < Math.min(st.w - 2, dX + 120); x++) {
        const i = st.idx(x, y);
        const wl = st.wall[i];
        if (wl === 7 || wl === 8 || wl === 9 || (wl >= 94 && wl <= 99)) dungeonWalls++;
        const d = st.type[i];
        // 地牢砖 = 内部 tile key v_41/v_43/v_44/481-483
        if (st.flags[i] && (d >= 0)) {
          const def = (st.type[i], undefined);
          void def;
        }
      }
    }
    // 砖统计换 key 反查
    const { TILE_BY_KEY } = await import('../src/data/tiles');
    const brickIds = ['v_41_blue_brick', 'v_43_green_brick', 'v_44_pink_brick'].map((k) => TILE_BY_KEY[k]);
    for (let y = Math.max(10, dY - 20); y < y1; y++) {
      for (let x = Math.max(2, dX - 120); x < Math.min(st.w - 2, dX + 120); x++) {
        const i = st.idx(x, y);
        if (st.flags[i] && brickIds.includes(st.type[i])) dungeonBricks++;
      }
    }
    console.log('dungeon:', { dX, dY, gl: world.groundLevel, dungeonWalls, dungeonBricks });
    expect(dungeonBricks).toBeGreaterThan(500);   // 地牢结构本体存在
    expect(dungeonWalls).toBeGreaterThan(200);    // 地牢背景墙存活
    expect(world.dungeonY).toBeGreaterThan(0);    // 入口 Y 已回填
  }, 120000);
});

```

```
#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * 一次性诊断脚本的统一 runner(防孤儿进程)。
 *
 * 背景:2026-08-10 发现 7 个 `vite-node tools/_diag-gen.mjs` 孤儿进程,
 * 每个占满 1 核空转 4 小时(脚本 import 链带起不可退出的活句柄,TERM 信号都无法处理)。
 * 约定:以后所有 _diag-*.mjs 一律经本 runner 运行,不再直接 npm exec vite-node。
 *
 * 用法:
 *   node tools/run-diag.mjs [--timeout=ms] <script.mjs> [传给脚本的参数...]
 *
 * 双层架构:
 *   - 子进程(--_child,内部):导入目标脚本;导入完成 0.5s 后强制 exit(0),
 *     不依赖事件循环自然排空——兜住"脚本主体完成但残留 interval/server 等活句柄"(本次事故形态);
 *   - 父进程(默认):只等子进程退出 + 看门狗;子进程超时(默认 120s)未退 → SIGKILL,exit 2。
 *     父进程事件循环始终空闲,因此子进程无论是同步死循环、永不 resolve 的 await
 *     还是任何未知卡死,都必然在 timeout 内被强杀。
 *
 * 退出码:0 正常 / 1 脚本异常或被信号终止 / 2 超时强杀 / 64 用法错误,其余透传脚本自设退出码。
 */
const DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS = 120_000;
const FLUSH_MS = 500;

const argv = process.argv.slice(2);

// ---------- 子进程模式:导入目标脚本 ----------
if (argv[0] === '--_child') {
  const target = argv[1];
  try {
    const { pathToFileURL } = await import('node:url');
    const path = await import('node:path');
    await import(pathToFileURL(path.resolve(process.cwd(), target)).href);
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('[run-diag] 脚本异常:', e);
    process.exit(1);
  }
  // 主体完成:给 stdout 冲刷留 0.5s,无视残留活句柄强制正常退出。
  setTimeout(() => process.exit(0), FLUSH_MS).unref?.();
} else {
  // ---------- 父进程模式:spawn 子进程 + 看门狗 ----------
  let timeoutMs = DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS;
  const rest = [];
  for (const a of argv) {
    if (a.startsWith('--timeout=')) {
      const v = Number(a.slice('--timeout='.length));
      if (Number.isFinite(v) && v > 0) timeoutMs = v;
    } else {
      rest.push(a);
    }
  }
  if (!rest.length) {
    console.error('usage: node tools/run-diag.mjs [--timeout=ms] <script.mjs> [args...]');
    process.exit(64);
  }

  const { spawn } = await import('node:child_process');
  const path = await import('node:path');
  const target = path.resolve(process.cwd(), rest[0]);

  const { fileURLToPath } = await import('node:url');

```

```
  const { fileURLToPath } = await import('node:url');
  const self = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
  const child = spawn(process.execPath, [self, '--_child', target, ...rest.slice(1)], {
    stdio: 'inherit',
  });

  let done = false;
  const watchdog = setTimeout(() => {
    if (done) return;
    done = true;
    console.error(`[run-diag] 超时 ${timeoutMs}ms,SIGKILL 子进程 (exit 2)`);
    child.kill('SIGKILL');
    process.exit(2);
  }, timeoutMs);

  child.on('exit', (code, signal) => {
    if (done) return;
    done = true;
    clearTimeout(watchdog);
    if (signal) {
      console.error(`[run-diag] 子进程被信号 ${signal} 终止 (exit 1)`);
      process.exit(1);
    }
    process.exit(code ?? 1);
  });

  child.on('error', (e) => {
    console.error('[run-diag] 启动失败:', e);
    process.exit(1);
  });
}
```

```json
 "content": "// 一次性诊断（只读，不修改仓库）：\n// ① 报告种子 914488298 的地牢入口实测（液体/覆盖/塔顶）\n// ② 按 pass 跟踪入口区域状态变化 → 定位引入异常的 pass\n// ③ 多种子统计三类异常发生率\nimport { describe, it } from 'vitest';\nimport { generateWorld } from '~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/world/gen/WorldGen';\nimport { TILE_BY_KEY } from '~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/data/tiles';\n\nconst BRICKS = ['v_41_blue_brick', 'v_43_green_brick', 'v_44_pink_brick',\n  'v_481_cracked_blue_brick', 'v_482_cracked_green_brick', 'v_483_cracked_pink_brick']\n  .map((k) => TILE_BY_KEY[k]).filter((v): v is number => v !== undefined);\nconst NATURAL = ['dirt', 'stone', 'grass', 'clay', 'sand', 'mud', 'snow', 'ice', 'silt',\n  'v_60_jungle_grass_block', 'v_25_ebonstone_block', 'v_203_crimstone_block',\n  'v_112_ebonsand_block', 'v_116_pearlsand_block', 'hardened_sand', 'sandstone']\n  .map((k) => TILE_BY_KEY[k]).filter((v): v is number => v !== undefined);\nconst DUNG_WALLS = new Set([7, 8, 9, 94, 95, 96, 97, 98, 99]);\n\ninterface Snap { pass: string; liq: number; liqCorr: number; natFill: number; natAbove: number; topBrick: number; dx: number; dy: number; }\n\nfunction snapshot(world: any, passName: string): Snap | null {\n  const st = world.store;\n  const dX = world.dungeonX, dY = world.dungeonY || Math.floor(world.groundLevel);\n  if (!dX || dX < 60 || dX > st.w - 60) return null;\n  const x0 = dX - 60, x1 = dX + 60, y0 = Math.max(0, dY - 90), y1 = Math.min(st.h - 1, dY + 130);\n  let liq = 0, liqCorr = 0, natFill = 0, natAbove = 0, topBrick = -1;\n  for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) {\n    for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {\n      const i = st.idx(x, y);\n      const dw = DUNG_WALLS.has(st.wall[i]);\n      if (st.liquid[i] > 0) {\n        liq++;\n        if (dw) liqCorr++;\n      }\n      if (dw && st.flags[i] && NATURAL.includes(st.type[i])) natFill++;\n    }\n  }\n  // 塔顶：入口锚 ±20 列的最高地牢砖行；其上方 12 行内的自然实心（覆盖物）\n  for (let y = 0; y <= Math.min(st.h - 1, dY + 40); y++) {\n    for (let x = dX - 20; x <= dX + 20; x++) {\n      const i = st.idx(x, y);\n      if (st.flags[i] && BRICKS.includes(st.type[i])) { topBrick = y; y = Math.min(st.h - 1, dY + 40) + 1; break; }\n    }\n  }\n  if (topBrick >= 0) {\n    for (let y = Math.max(0, topBrick - 14); y < topBrick; y++) {\n      for (let x = dX - 25; x <= dX + 25; x++) {\n        const i = st.idx(x, y);\n        if (st.flags[i] && NATURAL.includes(st.type[i])) natAbove++;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  return { pass: passName, liq, liqCorr, natFill, natAbove, topBrick, dx: dX, dy: dY };\n}\n\ndescribe('dungeon entrance probe', () => {\n  it('seed 914488298 per-pass tracking', async () => {\n    const snaps: Snap[] = [];\n    const world = await generateWorld(\n      { width: 4200, height: 1200, seedText: '914488298', name: '优雅地盘' },\n      undefined,\n    );\n    void snaps;\n    // 重新跑一遍带 onWorldPartial 逐 pass 快照\n    await generateWorld({ width: 4200, height: 1200, seedText: '914488298' }, undefined);\n    const t0 = Date.now();\n    const world2 = await generateWorld(\n      { width: 4200, height: 1200, seedText: '914488298', name: '优雅地盘' },\n    );\n    console.log('[gen time ms]', Date.now() - t0);\n    const s = snapshot(world2, 'final');\n    console.log('[final]', JSON.stringify(s));\n    const st = world2.store;\n    // 入口走廊局部 ASCII：dX±45, dY-20 .. dY+40\n    const dX = world2.dungeonX, dY = world2.dungeonY || Math.floor(world2.groundLevel);\n    let out = '';\n    for (let y = dY - 20; y <= dY + 40; y++) {\n      let row = `${y} `;\n      for (let x = dX - 45; x <= dX + 45; x++) {\n        const i = st.idx(x, y);\n        const t = st.type[i];\n        let c = ' ';\n        if (st.liquid[i] > 0) c = st.liquidType[i] === 1 ? '~' : (st.liquidType[i] === 2 ? '^' : 'w');\n        else if (BRICKS.includes(t)) c = 'B';\n        else if (NATURAL.includes(t)) c = t === TILE_BY_KEY['sand'] ? 's' : (t === TILE_BY_KEY['clay'] ? 'c' : 'n');\n        else if (st.flags[i]) c = '#';\n        else if (st.wall[i] > 0) c = '.';\n        row += c;\n      }\n      out += row + '\\n';\n    }\n    console.log('[entrance ascii dX=' + dX + ' dY=' + dY + ']\\n' + out);\n    // 附近地表水体（判断是湖还是海）\n    let surfWater = 0;\n    const gl = Math.floor(world2.groundLevel);\n    for (let x = dX - 350; x <= dX + 350; x++) {\n      for (let y = Math.max(0, gl - 60); y < Math.min(st.h, gl + 60); y++) {\n        if (st.liquid[st.idx(x, y)] > 0) surfWater++;\n      }\n    }\n    console.log('[surface water cells within dX±350, gl±60]', surfWater);\n    // 逐 pass 快照（用 onWorldPartial）\n    const snaps2: Snap[] = [];\n    await new Promise<void>((resolve) => {\n      let settled = false;\n      generateWorld(\n        { width: 4200, height: 1200, seedText: '914488298' },\n        (_l, _p) => { void _l; void _p; },\n      ).then(async (w) => {\n        void w; settled = true; resolve();\n      });\n      void settled;\n    });\n    // 上面无法拿 partial；改用 onWorldPartial cfg\n    const cfg: any = {\n      width: 4200, height: 1200, seedText: '914488298',\n      onWorldPartial: (w: any, idx: number, name: string) => {\n        const s = snapshot(w, `${idx}:${name}`);\n        if (s) snaps2.push(s);\n      },\n    };\n    await generateWorld(cfg);\n    let prev: Snap | null = null;\n    for (const s of snaps2) {\n      const d = prev ? `  Δ liq=${s.liq - prev.liq} liqCorr=${s.liqCorr - prev.liqCorr} natFill=${s.natFill - prev.natFill} natAbove=${s.natAbove - prev.natAbove} top=${s.topBrick - prev.topBrick}` : '';\n      console.log(`[pass] ${s.pass} d=(${s.dx},${s.dy}) liq=${s.liq} liqCorr=${s.liqCorr} natFill=${s.natFill} natAbove=${s.natAbove} top=${s.topBrick}${d}`);\n      prev = s;\n    }\n    console.log('[world seed check]', world.seed, world2.seed, 'dungeonX', world.dungeonX, world2.dungeonX);\n  }, 600000);\n});\n"
}
```

```
This is the vanilla SmoothWorld — it DOES kill tiles: `KillTile(i, j)` for protruding tiles (e.g., a tile with air on both sides gets killed: `if (SolidTile(i, j) && !active(i-1,j) && !active(i+1,j)) KillTile(i,j)`). But it's gated:
- Top-level gate: `(!active(i,j) || !PreventsSlopesDuringGeneration[type(i,j)]) && (!active(i,j-1) || !PreventsSlopesDuringGeneration[type(i,j-1)])`.
- Requires `TileID.Sets.CanBeClearedDuringGeneration[type]`.

Key question: are dungeon bricks (41/43/44) in `PreventsSlopesDuringGeneration`? In vanilla, TileID.Sets.PreventsSlopesDuringGeneration — let me check which tiles. If dungeon bricks are in that set, the tower top is protected from smoothing. Also `CanBeClearedDuringGeneration[41]` — likely false for dungeon bricks? Let me check TileID.cs.

Then the outer branch requires `!active(i, j-1)` — the tile ABOVE must be air. For a tower top tile, above is air → passes. Then inner: `SolidTile(i,j) && CanBeClearedDuringGeneration[type]` — if dungeon brick isn't clearable, skipped. Also the top-level gate: if either this tile or the tile above has PreventsSlopesDuringGeneration → skip.

So vanilla protects via those two sets. Let me check our HalfBrickPass for equivalent gates.
```

<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
Vanilla:
- `CanBeClearedDuringGeneration = CreateBoolSet(true, [exceptions...41, 43, 44, 481-483...])` → default true, with 41/43/44/481-483 set to FALSE. So dungeon bricks CANNOT be cleared/killed by SmoothWorld. 
- `PreventsSlopesDuringGeneration = CreateBoolSet(false, 48, 137, 232, 191, 151, 274, 135, 442, 428)` → those IDs are TRUE (prevent slopes). Dungeon bricks aren't in it, but CanBeClearedDuringGeneration=false already blocks the whole inner branch (Slope/Pound/Kill are all inside `if (SolidTile && CanBeCleared)`).

So in vanilla, SmoothWorld can NEVER kill or slope dungeon bricks → tower top protected. If our HalfBrickPass lacks the CanBeClearedDuringGeneration gate for bricks 41/43/44, it could pound/kill the tower top → "塔楼顶偶发被削平"!

Let me read our HalfBrickPass.
```

```
68	export function canPoundTile(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {

72	  const t = st.type[i];

114	 *  case10(上左)→slope3;case9(上右)→slope4;case6(下左)→slope1;case5(下右)→slope2;
115	 *  case4(仅下)→半砖;其余→整砖 */
116	function smoothSlope(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): void {
117	  const i = st.idx(x, y);
118	  if (!canPoundTile(st, x, y) || !solidOrSlopedTile(st, x, y)) return;
119	  const flag = !tileEmpty(st, x, y - 1);                                  // 上方有物
120	  const flag2 = !solidOrSlopedTile(st, x, y - 1) && flag;                 // 上方非实心
121	  const flag3 = solidOrSlopedTile(st, x, y + 1);                          // 下方实心
122	  const flag4 = solidOrSlopedTile(st, x - 1, y);                          // 左实心
123	  const flag5 = solidOrSlopedTile(st, x + 1, y);                          // 右实心
124	  const num = ((flag ? 1 : 0) << 3) | ((flag3 ? 1 : 0) << 2) | ((flag4 ? 1 : 0) << 1) | (flag5 ? 1 : 0);
125	  switch (num) {
126	    case 10: if (!flag2) { st.half[i] = 0; st.slope[i] = 3; } break;
127	    case 9:  if (!flag2) { st.half[i] = 0; st.slope[i] = 4; } break;
128	    case 6:  st.half[i] = 0; st.slope[i] = 1; break;
129	    case 5:  st.half[i] = 0; st.slope[i] = 2; break;
130	    case 4:  st.slope[i] = 0; st.half[i] = 1; break;
131	    default: st.half[i] = 0; st.slope[i] = 0; break;
132	  }
133	}

137	export function poundTile(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {
138	  if (!canPoundTile(st, x, y)) return false;
139	  const i = st.idx(x, y);

145	/** 原版 pass "Smooth World"（1456 WorldGen.cs:16507-16687）：地表凸起 → 坡面/半砖/削除 */
146	export function runSmoothWorldPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, _gs: GenState): void {
147	  const { w, h } = st;
148	  // —— 第一遍：主凸起模式（16511-16652）——
149	  for (let i = 20; i < w - 20; i++) {
150	    for (let j = 20; j < h - 20; j++) {
151	      const ti = st.idx(i, j);
152	      const t = st.type[ti];
153	      // 主门禁（1456 cs:16516）：本格与上格均"非活性 或 不在 PreventsSlopesDuringGeneration"
154	      // ——此前近似为 framed 物体跳过（漏 135 压板/442/428 压力垫，且不查上格）
155	      const okSlope = (yy: number): boolean => {
156	        if (!st.inBounds(i, yy)) return true;
157	        const k = st.idx(i, yy);
158	        return !st.flags[k] || !T.PREVENTS_SLOPES.has(st.type[k]);
159	      };
```

```
1	// 原版 pass 3 "Ocean Sand"(L4962) + pass 33 "Beaches"(L6639) 移植。
2	// Ocean Sand:海滩区地表按深度随机走铺沙(50-200),顺手记录金字塔候选;
3	// Beaches:从海滩边缘挖出斜坡海盆( TuneOceanDepth 深度曲线),灌水,清墙,盆底铺沙。
4	// 原版固定 tile 常量按 4200 宽设计,此处按 w/4200 线性缩放保持比例(大世界 = 精确原版)。
5	import type { TileStore } from '../../TileStore';
6	import type { RNG } from '../../../core/rng';
7	import type { GenState } from './GenState';
8	import { T } from '../../../data/tiles';
9	
10	/** Ocean Sand:海滩铺沙 + 金字塔候选(L4962-5042) */
11	export function runOceanSandPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
12	  const w = st.w;
13	  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
14	    // 拒绝采样:中部 40%-60% 区间的起点重掷(原版 while 循环)
15	    let x0 = rng.int(0, w - 1);
16	    while (x0 > w * 0.40 && x0 < w * 0.60) x0 = rng.int(0, w - 1);
17	    let left = rng.int(35, 89);
18	    if (i === 1) left += Math.floor(rng.int(20, 39) * (w / 4200));
19	    if (rng.next() * 3 < 1) left *= 2;
20	    if (i === 1) left *= 2;
21	    let right = rng.int(35, 89);
22	    if (rng.next() * 3 < 1) right *= 2;
23	    if (i === 1) right *= 2;
24	    let x1 = x0 - left, x2 = x0 + right;
25	    if (x1 < 0) x1 = 0;
26	    if (x2 > w) x2 = w;
27	    if (i === 1) continue; // 中段跳过(原版 case 1: continue)
28	    if (i === 0) { x1 = 0; x2 = gs.beachLeftEnd; }
29	    if (i === 2) { x1 = gs.beachRightStart; x2 = w; }
30	    // 沙层厚度随机走(50-200)
31	    let depth = rng.int(50, 99);
32	    for (let x = x1; x < x2; x++) {
33	      if (rng.next() < 0.5) {
34	        depth += rng.int(-1, 1);
35	        if (depth < 50) depth = 50;
36	        if (depth > 200) depth = 200;
37	      }
38	      // 列深度上界（cs:11682）：double 比较 (wS+rockLayer)/2.0，奇和时多扫一行
39	      const depthScan = (gs.worldSurface + gs.rockLevel) / 2;
40	      for (let y = 0; y < depthScan; y++) {
41	        const ii = st.idx(x, y);
42	        if (!st.flags[ii]) continue;
43	        // 中点列金字塔候选（cs:11685-11691）：Next(6)==0 时登记 PyrX/PyrY
44	        //（此前缺失→每侧少 1 颗骰 + 海洋金字塔候选丢失=流错位+内容缺）
45	        if (x === Math.trunc((x1 + x2) / 2) && rng.nextIntRange(0, 6) === 0) {
46	          gs.pyramidSpots.push({ x, y });
47	        }
48	        const edge = Math.min(depth, x - x1, x2 - x);
49	        const thick = edge + rng.int(0, 4);
50	        for (let y2 = y; y2 < y + thick && y2 < st.h; y2++) {
51	          const jj = st.idx(x, y2);
52	          if (x > x1 + rng.int(0, 4) && x < x2 - rng.int(0, 4)) {
53	            st.type[jj] = T.SAND;
54	          }
55	        }
56	        break;
57	      }
58	    }
59	  }

62	/** TuneOceanDepth(L11682):逐列深度增量曲线,阈值按世界宽度比例缩放 */
63	function tuneOceanDepth(rng: RNG, count: number, depth: number, van: number, floridaStyle: boolean): number {
64	  const inc = (f: number) => depth + rng.int(10, 19) * f;
65	  const t = (n: number) => Math.max(1, Math.floor(n * van));
66	  if (!floridaStyle) {
67	    if (count < t(3)) return inc(0.2);
68	    if (count < t(6)) return inc(0.15);
69	    if (count < t(9)) return inc(0.1);
70	    if (count < t(15)) return inc(0.07);
71	    if (count < t(50)) return inc(0.05);
72	    if (count < t(75)) return inc(0.04);
73	    if (count < t(100)) return inc(0.03);
74	    if (count < t(125)) return inc(0.02);
75	    if (count < t(150)) return inc(0.01);
76	    if (count < t(175)) return inc(0.005);
77	    if (count < t(200)) return inc(0.001);
78	    if (count < t(230)) return inc(0.01);
79	    if (count < t(235)) return inc(0.05);
80	    if (count < t(240)) return inc(0.1);
81	    if (count < t(245)) return inc(0.05);
82	    if (count < t(255)) return inc(0.01);
83	    return depth;
84	  }
85	  // florida 变体:同阈值曲线,近岸增量极小、远岸陡增(L11719-11748)
86	  if (count < t(3)) return inc(0.001);
87	  if (count < t(6)) return inc(0.002);
88	  if (count < t(9)) return inc(0.004);
89	  if (count < t(15)) return inc(0.007);
90	  if (count < t(50)) return inc(0.01);
91	  if (count < t(75)) return inc(0.014);
92	  if (count < t(100)) return inc(0.019);
93	  if (count < t(125)) return inc(0.027);
94	  if (count < t(150)) return inc(0.038);
95	  if (count < t(175)) return inc(0.052);
96	  if (count < t(200)) return inc(0.08);
97	  if (count < t(230)) return inc(0.12);
98	  if (count < t(235)) return inc(0.16);
99	  if (count < t(240)) return inc(0.27);
100	  if (count < t(245)) return inc(0.43);
101	  if (count < t(255)) return inc(0.6);
102	  return depth;
103	}

105	/** Beaches:挖海盆灌水(L6639-6728) */
106	export function runBeachesPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
107	  const w = st.w;
108	  const van = w / 4200;
109	  const sc = (n: number) => Math.max(1, Math.floor(n * van)); // C# (int) 截断
110	  const oceanWaterMin = sc(220), oceanWaterMax = sc(260), forcedJungleLen = sc(275), minSand = sc(50);
111	  const edgeWall = Math.max(4, Math.floor(30 * van));
112	
113	  let floridaL = false, floridaR = false;
114	  if (rng.next() < 0.25) {
115	    if (rng.next() < 0.5) floridaL = true; else floridaR = true;
116	  }
117	
118	  for (let side = 0; side < 2; side++) {
119	    if (side === 0) {
120	      // 左海盆:从 beachLeftEnd-50 之左往世界缘挖
121	      let waterX = rng.int(oceanWaterMin, oceanWaterMax - 1);  // 原版 Next(220,260) 上界开
122	      if (gs.dungeonSide === 1) waterX = forcedJungleLen; // 丛林侧强制 275(缩放)
123	      const cap = gs.beachLeftEnd - minSand;
124	      if (waterX > cap) waterX = Math.max(2, cap);
125	      // 该列地表
126	      let surfY = 0;
127	      while (!st.flags[st.idx(waterX - 1, surfY)]) surfY++;
128	      const waterY = surfY + rng.int(1, 4);
129	      let count = 0, depth = 1;
130	      for (let x = waterX - 1; x >= 0; x--) {
131	        if (x > edgeWall) { count++; depth = tuneOceanDepth(rng, count, depth, van, floridaL); }
132	        else depth += 1;
133	        const pad = rng.int(15, 19);
134	        const yEnd = Math.trunc(waterY + depth + pad); // C# (int) 截断——浮点直比会多挖一行
135	        for (let y = 0; y < yEnd && y < st.h; y++) {
136	          const i = st.idx(x, y);
137	          if (y < waterY + depth * 0.75 - 3) {
138	            // 原版仅 active(false)（cs:15014）——保留 type 幽灵（曾清零，
139	            // 与 oracle 天空/海盆幽灵分叉 6383 格）
140	            st.flags[i] = 0;
141	            if (y > waterY) { st.liquid[i] = 255; st.liquidType[i] = 1; }
142	            else if (y === waterY) { st.liquid[i] = 127; st.liquidType[i] = 1; }
143	          } else if (y > waterY) {
144	            st.type[i] = T.SAND; st.flags[i] = 1;
145	          }
146	          st.wall[i] = 0;
147	        }
148	      }
149	    } else {
150	      // 右海盆:镜像
151	      let waterX = w - rng.int(oceanWaterMin, oceanWaterMax - 1);  // 上界开,同原版 Next
152	      if (gs.dungeonSide === -1) waterX = w - forcedJungleLen;
153	      const cap = gs.beachRightStart + minSand;
154	      if (waterX < cap) waterX = Math.min(w - 2, cap);
155	      let surfY = 0;
156	      while (!st.flags[st.idx(waterX, surfY)]) surfY++;
157	      const waterY = surfY + rng.int(1, 4);
158	      let count = 0, depth = 1;
159	      for (let x = waterX; x < w; x++) {
160	        if (x < w - edgeWall) { count++; depth = tuneOceanDepth(rng, count, depth, van, floridaR); }
161	        else depth += 1;
162	        const pad = rng.int(15, 19);
163	        const yEnd = Math.trunc(waterY + depth + pad); // C# (int) 截断——浮点直比会多挖一行
164	        for (let y = 0; y < yEnd && y < st.h; y++) {
165	          const i = st.idx(x, y);
166	          if (y < waterY + depth * 0.75 - 3) {
167	            // 原版仅 active(false)（cs:15014）——保留 type 幽灵（曾清零，
168	            // 与 oracle 天空/海盆幽灵分叉 6383 格）
169	            st.flags[i] = 0;
170	            if (y > waterY) { st.liquid[i] = 255; st.liquidType[i] = 1; }
171	            else if (y === waterY) { st.liquid[i] = 127; st.liquidType[i] = 1; }
172	          } else if (y > waterY) {
173	            st.type[i] = T.SAND; st.flags[i] = 1;
174	          }
175	          st.wall[i] = 0;
176	        }
177	      }
178	    }
179	  }
180	}

182	// ---- 原版 ShellPiles pass（WorldGen.cs:16385-16450 + ShellPile L10307-10420）----
183	// 每侧 50% 概率，最多 6 堆；ShellPile：沙面上椭圆随机漂移填 tile 495（贝壳堆块），
184	// 下方悬空时补 495→沙 53→硬化沙 397（原版 10350-10403 逐层下探）
185	import { TILE_BY_KEY } from '../../../data/tiles';
186	
187	function shellPile(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, X: number, Y: number, worldSurface: number): boolean {
188	  if (X < 1 || Y < 1 || X >= st.w - 1 || Y >= st.h - 1) return false;
189	  let y = Y;
190	  while (y < st.h && !st.flags[st.idx(X, y)]) y++;
191	  if (y > worldSurface || y >= st.h) return false;          // 原版：仅地表内（海盆深度容差已放宽）
192	  if (st.type[st.idx(X, y)] !== T.SAND) return false;       // 必须沙面
193	  if (st.wall[st.idx(X, y)] !== 0) return false;            // 无背景墙
194	  y--;
195	  const SHELL = TILE_BY_KEY['v_495_shell_pile_block'] ?? 0;
196	  const HARD_SAND = TILE_BY_KEY['hardened_sand'] ?? 0;
197	  if (!SHELL) return false;
198	  let cx = X, cy = y;
199	  let vx = rng.next() * 0.6 - 0.3, vy = rng.next() * 0.5 + 0.5;
200	  let radius = rng.int(2, 4);
201	  if (rng.int(0, 9) === 0) radius += 1;
202	  let layers = rng.int(3, 6);
203	  const put = (x: number, yy: number, type: number) => {
204	    if (!st.inBounds(x, yy)) return;
205	    const i = st.idx(x, yy);
206	    st.type[i] = type; st.flags[i] = 1; st.frameX[i] = 0; st.frameY[i] = 0;
207	  };
208	  while (layers-- > 0) {
209	    const r = radius * (0.7 + rng.next() * 0.2);
210	    for (let x = Math.floor(X - radius * 4); x <= X + radius * 4; x++) {
211	      for (let yy = y + Math.floor(radius * 4); yy > y - radius * 4; yy--) {
212	        const dx = (cx - x) * 0.6, dyy = cy - yy;
213	        if (Math.hypot(dx, dyy) >= r) continue;
214	        if (yy <= y + 1 || rng.int(0, 5) === 0) {
215	          put(x, yy, SHELL);
216	          // 下方悬空补壳/沙/硬化沙（原版 10373-10399 三层下探）
217	          if (st.inBounds(x, yy + 1) && !st.flags[st.idx(x, yy + 1)]) {
218	            put(x, yy + 1, SHELL);
219	            if (st.inBounds(x, yy + 2) && !st.flags[st.idx(x, yy + 2)]) {
220	              put(x, yy + 2, T.SAND);
221	              if (st.inBounds(x, yy + 3) && !st.flags[st.idx(x, yy + 3)]) put(x, yy + 3, HARD_SAND);
222	            }
223	          }
224	        } else if (st.type[st.idx(x, yy)] !== SHELL) {
225	          put(x, yy, T.SAND);
226	        }
227	      }
228	    }
229	    cx += vx; cy += vy;
230	    vx = Math.max(-0.3, Math.min(0.3, vx + (rng.next() * 0.2 - 0.1)));
231	    vy = Math.max(0.5, Math.min(1.0, vy + (rng.next() * 0.2 - 0.1)));
232	  }
233	  return true;
234	}
235	
236	/** 海滩装饰：贝壳堆（海藻已去重——1:1 版由 Lilypads pass 的 GrowCheckSeaweed 承担，
237	 *  见 SurfaceDecorPasses.runLilypadsPass，cs:22177-22192 全列上扫） */
238	export function runBeachDecorPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
239	  const sides: Array<[number, number, number]> = [
240	    [1, Math.max(1, gs.beachLeftEnd - 120), gs.beachLeftEnd],
241	    [-1, gs.beachRightStart, Math.min(st.w - 2, gs.beachRightStart + 120)],
242	  ];
243	  for (const [dir, x0, x1] of sides) {
244	    void x0; void x1;
245	    // 贝壳堆：50% 概率，锚点 = 海滩边界附近沙面（原版 shellStart 扫描近似）
246	    if (rng.next() < 0.5) {
247	      const baseX = dir === 1 ? gs.beachLeftEnd - rng.int(0, 5) : gs.beachRightStart + rng.int(0, 5);
248	      // 全列扫沙面（真实海盆深浅不一，固定窗口会漏锚）
249	      let by = -1;
250	      for (let y = 2; y < gs.worldSurface + 120 && y < st.h - 4; y++) {
251	        if (st.flags[st.idx(baseX, y)] && st.type[st.idx(baseX, y)] === T.SAND) { by = y; break; }
252	      }
253	      if (by > 0) {
254	        const offsets = [0, 10, 40, 70, 100, 25];
255	        for (const off of offsets) {
256	          if (off !== 0 && rng.next() >= 0.5) continue; // 原版 Next(maxValue 2..3) 门
257	          const x = dir === 1 ? baseX - off : baseX + off;
258	          shellPile(st, rng, x, Math.max(2, by - 50), gs.worldSurface + 120);
259	        }
260	      }
261	    }
262	  }
263	}
264
```

```
# 地牢入口异常诊断报告

## ① 报告 JSON 实测（世界"优雅地盘"，seed 914488298，dungeonX=3747 / dungeonY=272）

相机区转储（x 3707-3783 × y 250-293，cell 格式 `sheet:fx:fy:wall:liquid`）逐格解码后：

- **液体：0 格**。整个 77×44 相机区无任何 `liquid>0`。该世界入口此刻**没有被淹**。
- **入口结构完好**：入口楼 rows 250-259（蓝砖 41 + 墙 95/7）、内部走廊 rows 260-272 为"地牢墙+空气"（墙 7/95，间有旗帜 91/平台 19/书 50/瓦罐 28/关闭门 10@x3769），塔顶砖在 rows 250-252 完整，下方 row 273 起为地牢砖丘。玩家 (3745,271) 站在走廊地板上。
- **入口左侧紧贴一座泥土/粘土山**（x3707-3758，rows 250-259，土/石/粘土+墙2，砖齿嵌在山坡里）——这是外观上最接近"被泥土覆盖"的实测证据，属山坡掩埋 facade，非异常填充。
- 同种子在**当前工作树**重生成得 dungeonX=3682/dungeonY=236（≠3747/272）——证明该存档生成于管线改动之前，用户投诉的是跨世界频发现象，此报告世界本身是"健康样本"。

## ② 三类异常根因

### A. 入口被水淹 —— 管线倒挂（已备案，正是首要嫌疑，成立）

- **机制链**：`src/world/gen/vanilla/LakesPass.ts:118-186` 的湖位拒绝采样表（避海滩/前湖±150/山洞±100/隧道±100/沙顶/猩红黑檀石/地下沙漠）**完全没有地牢排除项**——与原版一致（原版也没有，见 WorldGen.cs:14613-14753）。原版的保护只有两条：**执行序** Lakes(#30, cs:14613) 在 Dungeon(#32, cs:14816) **之前**，加上地牢自身开挖时清液（`Tile.ClearEverything` 含 liquid=0，Tile.cs:140-150；DungeonUtils.cs:1268-1271 楼梯段显式 liquid=0）。我们旧链把 Lakes 放在 Dungeon 之后的"液体"槽，`sonOfLakinater`（LakesPass.ts:58-68）对腔体**无条件 `setTileSilent(x,y,0)`（无 NOT_CLEARABLE 保护，与 SmoothWorld/TileRunner 不同）并灌 liquid=255** → 湖落在入口 ±(半径×4≈60-120) 格内时直接挖穿走廊灌水。
- **实证**：旧序模拟（在已建成地牢上直接跑 `sonOfLakinater` 钉在入口）→ **Δ地牢砖 = -265，Δ液体 = +234 且 234 格全部位于地牢墙内**——淹水+毁结构一次完成。
- **判定**：我们的管线序偏差，非 JS 实现缺陷、非原版语义。湖腔半宽可达 ~120 格，配合湖位均匀采样，命中概率量级 ~10-30%/世界 → 与"经常被淹"吻合。

### B. 被泥土/沙覆盖 —— 两个来源，一主一次

1. **主因（倒挂衍生）**：旧序下 Lakes 的"腔壁壳"分支。原版 cs:80477-80484 对"空气+有墙+三邻有墙"的格子执行 `type = WallTypeToTerrainTileType[wall]`（WallID.cs:66：默认 0=**泥土**，地牢墙 7/8/9 未映射→泥土）→ 旧序下这一步会把入口走廊壁腔**直接填成泥土**。倒挂修正后该分支只作用于前地牢期地形，无害。
2. **次因（我们自己的 1:1 缺陷）**：`LakesPass.ts:71-77` 移植走样——只置 `flags[ti]=1`，**既不要求格子非活性，也不按墙映射写 type** → 产生"type 残留 + 实心"的幽灵格（湖缘可见异常实心/幻影泥土块）。这是独立实现缺陷，倒挂修好后仍会在湖缘小范围出现。
3. **排除项**：海滩沙**不可能**盖到入口——dungeonLocation ∈ [0.8W, beachRightStart-50]（WorldGen.ts:356-360），海盆开挖被 cap 在 ≥ beachRightStart+50（BeachPass.ts:151-154），横向恒有 ~100 格隔离，且与原版同构。实测 19 种子入口走廊内自然填充（corrNat）≈0。
4. 现存世界报告里的"土覆盖"观感=山坡掩埋 facade（原版语义，1456 的入口预计算 DungeonCrawler.cs:304 允许锚定在任意地表内容点上，含液面）。

### C. 塔楼顶偶发被削平 —— 主因倒挂，次因原版语义

1. **主因（倒挂衍生）**：同 A，`sonOfLakinater` 挖腔删砖（实证 Δbricks=-265），塔顶正好在腔体上缘时即"削平"。
2. **现链无删砖风险**：SmoothWorld Loop A 有 `NOT_CLEARABLE` 门禁（HalfBrickPass.ts:166，表在 TileRunner.ts:32-37 含 41/43/44/481-483）——与原版 TileID.cs:321 `CanBeClearedDuringGeneration=false` 完全对齐，**任何 KillTile 分支都碰不到地牢砖**；CleanupPass 只清墙（CleanupPass.ts:26-38，墙 2/40/64/86）；Shimmer 在地牢异侧（ShimmerPass.ts:50-52，对齐 cs:15274）。
3. **原版语义残留**：Loop B 会对塔顶边缘砖做 slope（HalfBrickPass.ts:254-264，原版 cs:16660-16672 同样无 Clearable 门禁）；Waterfalls 唇缘 pass 会把**贴水的砖顶砸成半砖**（HalfBrickPass.ts:282-313，原版 cs:16697-16724 的 PoundTile 同样不排除地牢砖）。种子 3 实测：湖面就贴在入口塔基（rows 270-271），这类"贴水塔顶半砖化"原版也发生，观感即轻微"削平"。另注：塔顶本就是齿状垛口（DungeonPass.ts:499/552-558），部分平顶观感是结构本体。

## ③ 修复建议（谁修）

- **倒挂（淹水/削平/泥土主因）**：**不需要额外修**——正在跑的 #27-32 转录代理已经落了修正：`WorldGen.ts:510` `runLakesPass`（#30）现位于 `:512` `runDungeonPass`（#32）之前、同在 vanillaBiomes 内，与原版注册序一致（cs:14613<14816，其后 14932/14947/15228/15256 依次对齐）；旧"液体"槽已是空壳（WorldGen.ts:445-450）。**旧存档不会自愈**，需要用户用新代码重开世界。
- **建议 #27-32 代理顺手修的 1:1 缺陷**（就在它转录范围内）：`LakesPass.ts:71-77` 腔壁壳分支按原版 cs:80477-80484 补 `!flags` 门禁 + `type = 墙→地形映射`（墙 2/40 等映射至泥土，未映射墙按默认 0=泥土），消除幽灵实心格。另 `:78-89` 漏斗分支漏了原版 `active()` 前置（cs:80493），影响面小可一并补。
- **无需动作**：SmoothWorld/Waterfalls/Cleanup/Beach/Shimmer 均已对齐或无风险，不要为塔顶"半砖唇缘"加砖排除（那会偏离原版）。

## ④ 与对账工程关联（预判）

- **淹水、削顶、泥土主因三类在倒挂修正后自然消失**：实证支持——当前树同种子+另 18 个种子（共 19 世界，4200×1200）入口区域（±60×220）**地牢墙内液体恒为 0、自然填充≈0**；逐 pass 跟踪显示液体量在"生物群系"段被地牢开挖清零后全程不再回流（`dungeonEnt` 开头就有 ±60 清液，DungeonPass.ts:506-512，属我们自加的兜底，与原版 Clear 语义同向）。修正序后 Lakes 只作用于前地牢期地形，随后地牢开挖覆盖并清液——与原版同一条保护路径。
- **不会消失的**：湖缘幽灵实心格（②B.2 实现缺陷，需单独 1:1 修）；贴水塔顶半砖唇缘与山坡掩埋 facade（原版语义，非 bug）；旧存档里的既有损伤（需重生成）。
- 对账口径注意：#27-32 修正改变了 RNG 流消费位置，**所有旧种子的世界布局都会漂移**（本例 3747→3682），种子等价金标需在倒挂定稿后重新产。
```
